```
    43→
    44→
    45→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    46→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    47→
    48→    items: list[dict]
    49→    total: int
    50→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    51→    requested_tier: int | None
    52→    selected_tier: int | None
    53→    fallback_reason: str | None
    54→    available_tiers: list[int]
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _apply_plan_order(
    59→    items: list[dict],
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    *,
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    63→    cluster: str | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    66→
    67→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    68→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    69→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    70→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    71→    """
    72→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    73→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    74→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    75→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    76→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    77→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup
    80→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    81→    for item in items:
    82→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    83→
    84→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    85→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    86→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    87→        if override.get("description"):
    88→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    89→        if override.get("note"):
    90→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    91→        if override.get("cluster"):
    92→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    93→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    94→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    95→                "name": cluster_name,
    96→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    97→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    98→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    99→            }
   100→
   101→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   102→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   103→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   104→    for fid in queue_order:
   105→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   106→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   107→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   108→
   109→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   110→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   111→    for item in items:
   112→        item_id = item["id"]
   113→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   114→            continue
   115→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   116→            skipped_items.append(item)
   117→        else:
   118→            remaining.append(item)
   119→
   120→    # Assign queue positions
   121→    result = ordered + remaining
   122→    if include_skipped:
   123→        result = result + skipped_items
   124→
   125→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   126→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   127→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   128→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   129→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   130→            if skip_entry:
   131→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   132→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   133→                if skip_reason:
   134→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   135→
   136→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   137→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   138→    if effective_cluster:
   139→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   140→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   141→        if cluster_member_ids:
   142→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   143→
   144→    return result
   145→
   146→
   147→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   148→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   149→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   150→        return any(
   151→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   152→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   153→        )
   154→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   155→
   156→
   157→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   158→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   159→    try:
   160→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   161→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   162→        if meta:
   163→            return meta.action_type
   164→    except ImportError:
   165→        pass
   166→    return "manual_fix"
   167→
   168→
   169→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   170→
   171→
   172→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   174→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   175→    if not clusters:
   176→        return items
   177→
   178→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   179→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   180→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   181→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   182→            continue
   183→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   184→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   185→
   186→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   187→        return items
   188→
   189→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   190→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   191→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   192→
   193→    for item in items:
   194→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   195→        if cname:
   196→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   197→        else:
   198→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   199→
   200→    # Build cluster meta-items
   201→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   202→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   203→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   204→        if len(members) < 2:
   205→            result.extend(members)
   206→            continue
   207→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   208→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   209→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   210→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   211→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   212→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   213→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   214→            action_type = "reorganize"
   215→        else:
   216→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   217→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   218→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   219→                action_type = "refactor"
   220→
   221→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   222→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   223→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   224→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   225→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   226→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   227→        else:
   228→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   229→
   230→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   231→        if not primary_command:
   232→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   233→
   234→        meta_item: dict = {
   235→            "id": cname,
   236→            "kind": "cluster",
   237→            "action_type": action_type,
   238→            "summary": summary,
   239→            "members": members,
   240→            "member_count": len(members),
   241→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   242→            "cluster_name": cname,
   243→            "cluster_auto": True,
   244→            "confidence": "high",
   245→            "detector": detector,
   246→            "file": "",
   247→        }
   248→        result.append(meta_item)
   249→
   250→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   251→    return result
   252→
   253→
   254→def build_work_queue(
   255→    state: StateModel,
   256→    *,
   257→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   258→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   259→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   260→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   261→
   262→    status = resolved_options.status
   263→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   264→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   265→    try:
   266→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   267→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   268→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   269→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   270→
   271→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   272→        state,
   273→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   274→        status_filter=status,
   275→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   276→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   277→    )
   278→
   279→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   280→    if (
   281→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   282→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   283→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   284→    ):
   285→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   286→            state,
   287→            state.get("findings", {}),
   288→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   289→        )
   290→        for item in subjective_items:
   291→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   292→                all_items.append(item)
   293→
   294→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   295→
   296→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   297→    if resolved_options.plan:
   298→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   299→            all_items,
   300→            resolved_options.plan,
   301→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   302→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   303→        )
   304→
   305→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   306→    should_collapse = (
   307→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   308→        and resolved_options.plan
   309→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   310→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   311→    )
   312→    if should_collapse:
   313→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   314→
   315→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   316→
   317→    requested_tier = (
   318→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   319→    )
   320→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   321→    fallback_reason = None
   322→    filtered = all_items
   323→
   324→    if requested_tier is not None:
   325→        filtered = [
   326→            item
   327→            for item in all_items
   328→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   329→        ]
   330→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   331→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   332→            if chosen is not None:
   333→                selected_tier = chosen
   334→                filtered = [
   335→                    item
   336→                    for item in all_items
   337→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   338→                ]
   339→                fallback_reason = (
   340→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   341→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   342→                )
   343→        elif not filtered:
   344→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   345→
   346→    total = len(filtered)
   347→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   348→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   349→
   350→    if resolved_options.explain:
   351→        for item in filtered:
   352→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   353→
   354→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   355→    return {
   356→        "items": filtered,
   357→        "total": total,
   358→        "tier_counts": counts,
   359→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   360→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   361→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   362→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   363→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   364→    }
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   369→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   370→    "WorkQueueResult",
   371→    "build_work_queue",
   372→    "group_queue_items",
   373→]
   374→
```

```
    53→    *,
    54→    patterns: list[str] | None = None,
    55→    status: str | None = None,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    58→    if not attestation:
    59→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    60→    elif missing:
    61→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    62→        _emit_warning(
    63→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    64→        )
    65→    _emit_warning(
    66→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    67→    )
    68→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    69→    if patterns and status:
    70→        pattern_args = " ".join(f'"{p}"' for p in patterns)
    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f'\nCopy-paste:\n  desloppify resolve {status} {pattern_args} --attest "{example}"',
    74→                "dim",
    75→            ),
    76→            file=sys.stderr,
    77→        )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    81→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    82→        _emit_warning(
    83→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    84→        )
    85→        sys.exit(1)
    86→    if args.status == "open":
    87→        return
    88→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    89→        show_attestation_requirement(
    90→            "Manual resolve",
    91→            attestation,
    92→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    93→            patterns=getattr(args, "patterns", None),
    94→            status=args.status,
    95→        )
    96→        sys.exit(1)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   100→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   101→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   105→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
   106→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   107→    for pattern in patterns:
   108→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   109→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
   110→            if finding_id:
   111→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
   112→    return len(matched_ids)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   116→    state: dict,
   117→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   118→    *,
   119→    attestation: str | None,
   120→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   121→) -> float:
   122→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   123→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   124→    if before is None:
   125→        return 0.0
   126→
   127→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   128→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   129→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   130→    if after is None:
   131→        return 0.0
   132→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   133→
   134→
   135→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   136→    state: dict,
   137→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   138→    *,
   139→    attestation: str | None,
   140→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   141→) -> None:
   142→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   146→    if preview_count <= 10:
   147→        return
   148→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   152→        state,
   153→        args,
   154→        attestation=attestation,
   155→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   156→    )
   157→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} findings).")
   158→    if strict_delta > 0:
   159→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   160→    _emit_warning(
   161→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   162→    )
   163→    sys.exit(1)
   164→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    88→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
    89→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
    90→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
    91→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
    92→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
    93→        )
    94→        return (
    95→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
    96→            action_pri,
    97→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
    98→            item.get("id", ""),
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   102→        return (
   103→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
   104→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
   105→            subjective_score_value(item),
   106→            item.get("id", ""),
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   110→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   111→    return (
   112→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   113→        0,
   114→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   115→        -review_weight,
   116→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   117→        item.get("id", ""),
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   122→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   123→
   124→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   125→        return {
   126→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   127→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   128→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   129→            "policy": (
   130→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   131→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   132→            ),
   133→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   134→        }
   135→
   136→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   137→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   138→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   139→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   140→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   141→    if is_subjective:
   142→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   143→    elif is_review:
   144→        ranking_factors = [
   145→            "tier asc",
   146→            "confidence asc",
   147→            "review_weight desc",
   148→            "count desc",
   149→            "id asc",
   150→        ]
   151→    else:
   152→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   153→    explain = {
   154→        "kind": "finding",
   155→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   156→        "confidence": confidence,
   157→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   158→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   159→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   160→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   161→    }
   162→    if is_review:
   163→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   164→    if is_subjective:
   165→        explain["policy"] = (
   166→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   167→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   168→        )
   169→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   170→    return explain
   171→
   172→
   173→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   174→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   175→    for item in items:
   176→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   177→            # Count each member at its own tier
   178→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   179→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   180→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   181→            continue
   182→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   183→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   184→    return counts
   185→
   186→
   187→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   188→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   189→    if not available:
   190→        return None
   191→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   192→
   193→
   194→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   195→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   196→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   197→    for item in items:
   198→        if group == "file":
   199→            key = item.get("file", "")
   200→        elif group == "detector":
   201→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   202→        elif group == "tier":
   203→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   204→        elif group == "cluster":
   205→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   206→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   207→        else:
   208→            key = "items"
   209→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   210→    return grouped
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "build_finding_items",
   215→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "tier_counts",
   219→    "subjective_score_value",
   220→    "group_queue_items",
   221→]
   222→
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_work_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    59→            tier=tier_filter,
    60→            count=None,
    61→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    62→            status="open",
    63→            include_subjective=True,
    64→            plan=plan_data,
    65→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    66→            cluster=effective_cluster,
    67→            collapse_clusters=True,
    68→        ),
    69→    )
    70→    items = queue.get("items", [])
    71→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    72→
    73→    # Header
    74→    total = len(items)
    75→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    76→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    77→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    78→    print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    79→
    80→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    81→    if focus:
    82→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    83→
    84→    if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    85→        # Check plan for skipped items not in queue
    86→        plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    87→        if plan_skipped_count:
    88→            print(colorize(
    89→                f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    90→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    91→                "dim",
    92→            ))
    93→
    94→    if not items:
    95→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
    96→        return
    97→
    98→    # Determine which items to show
    99→    display_items = items
   100→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   101→        display_items = items[:top]
   102→
   103→    # Build rows
   104→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   105→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   106→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   107→        pos = str(idx)
   108→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   109→
   110→        if kind == "cluster":
   111→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
   112→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   113→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   114→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   115→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   116→        else:
   117→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   118→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   119→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   120→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   121→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   122→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   123→
   124→        suffix = ""
   125→        if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   126→            suffix = " [skip]"
   127→
   128→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   129→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   130→
   131→    print()
   132→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   133→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   134→
   135→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   136→        remaining = len(items) - top
   137→        print(colorize(
   138→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   139→        ))
   140→    print()
   141→
   142→
   143→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   144→
```

```
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    72→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    73→            print(
    74→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    75→            )
    76→
    77→
    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    80→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    81→
    82→
    83→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    84→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    85→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    86→        return "[review]"
    87→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    88→        return "[auto]"
    89→    return ""
    90→
    91→
    92→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    93→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    94→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    95→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    96→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    97→}
    98→
    99→
   100→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   101→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   102→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   103→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   104→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   105→    if cluster_name == "auto/initial-review":
   106→        type_label = "Initial subjective review"
   107→    elif cluster_name == "auto/stale-review":
   108→        type_label = "Stale subjective review"
   109→    else:
   110→        type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
   111→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
   112→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   113→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   114→
   115→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
   116→    members = item.get("members", [])
   117→    if members:
   118→        # File distribution
   119→        from collections import Counter
   120→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   121→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   122→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   123→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   124→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   125→        else:
   126→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   127→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   128→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   129→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   130→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   131→
   132→        # Sample IDs
   133→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   134→        for m in members[:3]:
   135→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   136→        if len(members) > 3:
   137→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   138→
   139→    # Action
   140→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   141→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   142→    if primary_command:
   143→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   144→
   145→    # Resolution hints
   146→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify resolve fixed "{cluster_name}" '
   147→                   f'--note "<what>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"', "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   149→                   "dim"))
   150→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   151→
   152→
   153→def _render_item(
   154→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   155→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   158→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   159→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   163→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   164→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   165→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   166→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   167→
   168→    # Effort/type label
   169→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   170→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   171→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   172→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   173→
   174→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   175→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   176→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   177→        subjective_score = float(
   178→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   179→        )
   180→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   181→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   182→        print(
   183→            colorize(
   184→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   185→                "cyan",
   186→            )
   187→        )
   188→        print(colorize(
   189→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   190→            "dim",
   191→        ))
   192→        if explain:
   193→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   194→                "policy",
   195→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   196→            )
   197→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   201→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   202→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   203→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   204→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   205→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   206→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   207→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   208→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   209→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   210→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   211→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   212→
   213→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   214→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   215→
   216→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   217→    if detail.get("lines"):
   218→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   219→    if detail.get("category"):
   220→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   221→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   222→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   223→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   224→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   225→
   226→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   227→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   228→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   229→        if snippet:
   230→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   231→            print(snippet)
   232→
   233→    if dim_scores:
   234→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   235→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   236→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   237→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   238→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   239→            print(
   240→                colorize(
   241→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   242→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   243→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   244→                    "dim",
   245→                )
   246→            )
   247→
   248→    # Score impact estimate
   249→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   250→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   251→        try:
   252→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_score_impact
   253→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   254→            if impact > 0:
   255→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   256→            else:
   257→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   258→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   259→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   260→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   261→                    if issues > 1:
   262→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   263→                        if bulk > 0:
   264→                            print(colorize(
   265→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   266→                                "cyan",
   267→                            ))
   268→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   269→            pass
   270→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   271→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   272→        try:
   273→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   274→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   275→            if dim_key:
   276→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   277→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   278→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   279→                        continue
   280→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   281→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   282→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   283→                        if drag > 0.01:
   284→                            print(colorize(
   285→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   286→                                "cyan",
   287→                            ))
   288→                        break
   289→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   290→            pass
   291→
   292→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   293→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   294→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   295→        similar_count = sum(
   296→            1
   297→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   298→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   299→        )
   300→        if similar_count > 1:
   301→            print(
   302→                colorize(
   303→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   304→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   305→                    "cyan",
   306→                )
   307→            )
   308→    if explain:
   309→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   310→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   311→        base = (
   312→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   313→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   314→        )
   315→
   316→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   317→        if dim_scores and detector:
   318→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   319→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   320→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   321→                base += (
   322→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   323→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   324→                )
   325→
   326→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   327→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   328→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   329→            if dim_key:
   330→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   331→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   332→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   333→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   334→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   335→                        break
   336→
   337→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   338→        if policy:
   339→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   340→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   341→
   342→
   343→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   344→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   345→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   346→        return
   347→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   348→    if not explain:
   349→        return
   350→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   351→    if available:
   352→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   353→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   354→
   355→
   356→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   357→    if queue.get("items"):
   358→        return False
   359→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   360→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   361→    if tier is not None:
   362→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   363→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   364→        if available:
   365→            commands = " | ".join(
   366→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   367→            )
   368→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   369→    return True
   370→
   371→
   372→def render_terminal_items(
   373→    items: list[dict],
   374→    dim_scores: dict,
   375→    findings_scoped: dict,
   376→    *,
   377→    group: str,
   378→    explain: bool,
   379→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   380→    plan: dict | None = None,
   381→) -> None:
   382→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   383→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   384→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   385→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   386→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   387→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   388→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   389→
   390→    if group != "item":
   391→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   392→        return
   393→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   394→        if idx > 0:
   395→            print()
   396→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   397→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   398→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   399→        elif len(items) > 1:
   400→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   401→        else:
   402→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   403→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   404→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   405→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   406→
   407→
   408→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   409→    if len(items) != 1:
   410→        return
   411→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   412→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   413→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   414→        return
   415→    item = items[0]
   416→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   417→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   418→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   419→        primary = item.get(
   420→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   421→        )
   422→        print(f"    {primary}")
   423→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   424→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   425→        return
   426→
   427→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   428→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   429→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   430→        print(f"    {primary}")
   431→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   432→    else:
   433→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   434→
   435→    print(
   436→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   437→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   438→    )
   439→    print(
   440→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   441→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   442→    )
   443→
   444→
   445→def render_followup_nudges(
   446→    state: dict,
   447→    dim_scores: dict,
   448→    findings_scoped: dict,
   449→    *,
   450→    strict_score: float | None,
   451→    target_strict_score: float,
   452→) -> None:
   453→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   454→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   455→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   456→        state,
   457→        subjective_entries,
   458→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   459→        max_quality_items=3,
   460→        max_integrity_items=5,
   461→    )
   462→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   463→    if strict_score is not None:
   464→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   465→        if gap > 0:
   466→            print(
   467→                colorize(
   468→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   469→                    "cyan",
   470→                )
   471→            )
   472→        else:
   473→            print(
   474→                colorize(
   475→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   476→                    "green",
   477→                )
   478→            )
   479→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   480→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   481→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   482→
   483→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   484→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   485→        findings_scoped
   486→    )
   487→    parts: list[str] = []
   488→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   489→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   490→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   491→    open_review = [
   492→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   493→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   494→    ]
   495→    if low_dims:
   496→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   497→    if stale_count:
   498→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   499→    if unassessed_count:
   500→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   501→    if len(open_review):
   502→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   503→    if coverage_open > 0:
   504→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   505→
   506→    if parts:
   507→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   508→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   509→
   510→
   511→__all__ = [
   512→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   513→    "render_followup_nudges",
   514→    "render_queue_header",
   515→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   516→    "render_terminal_items",
   517→    "scorecard_subjective",
   518→    "show_empty_queue",
   519→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   520→]
   521→
```

```
    44→def _add_scan_parser(sub) -> None:
    45→    p_scan = sub.add_parser(
    46→        "scan",
    47→        help="Run all detectors, update state, show diff",
    48→        epilog="""\
    49→examples:
    50→  desloppify scan
    51→  desloppify scan --skip-slow
    52→  desloppify scan --profile ci
    53→  desloppify scan --force-resolve""",
    54→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    55→    )
    56→    p_scan.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
    57→    p_scan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
    58→    p_scan.add_argument(

    61→        help="Reset subjective measures to 0 before running scan",
    62→    )
    63→    p_scan.add_argument(

    72→    p_scan.add_argument(
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    33→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    34→    print(f"  Ordered items:    {ordered}")
    35→    if total_skipped:
    36→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    37→    else:
    38→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    39→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    40→    if clusters:
    41→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    42→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    43→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    45→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    46→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    47→    print(f"  Overrides:        {len(overrides)}")
    48→    if active:
    49→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    50→    if superseded:
    51→        print(f"  Superseded:       {superseded}")
    52→
    53→
    54→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    56→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    57→
    58→    plan = load_plan()
    59→    reset_plan(plan)
    60→    save_plan(plan)
    61→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    66→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    67→
    68→    if plan_action is None:
    69→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    if plan_action == "show":
    73→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if plan_action == "queue":
    77→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
    78→
    79→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    if plan_action == "reset":
    83→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action == "move":
    87→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    88→
    89→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    if plan_action in (
    93→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    94→        "skip", "unskip", "done", "reopen",
    95→    ):
    96→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    97→            cmd_plan_describe,
    98→            cmd_plan_done,
    99→            cmd_plan_focus,
   100→            cmd_plan_note,
   101→            cmd_plan_reopen,
   102→            cmd_plan_skip,
   103→            cmd_plan_unskip,
   104→        )
   105→
   106→        dispatch = {
   107→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   108→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   109→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   110→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   111→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   112→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   113→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   114→        }
   115→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   119→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   120→
   121→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   125→
   126→
   127→# Backwards-compatible alias
   128→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   129→
   130→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   131→
```

```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    41→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    42→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    43→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    44→
    45→
    46→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    47→    original_id: Required[str]
    48→    original_detector: str
    49→    original_file: str
    50→    original_summary: str
    51→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    52→    superseded_at: str
    53→    remapped_to: str | None
    54→    candidates: list[str]
    55→    note: str | None
    56→
    57→
    58→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    59→    version: Required[int]
    60→    created: Required[str]
    61→    updated: Required[str]
    62→    queue_order: list[str]
    63→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    65→    active_cluster: str | None
    66→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    67→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    68→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    69→
    70→
    71→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    72→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    73→    now = utc_now()
    74→    return {
    75→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    76→        "created": now,
    77→        "updated": now,
    78→        "queue_order": [],
    79→        "deferred": [],
    80→        "skipped": {},
    81→        "active_cluster": None,
    82→        "overrides": {},
    83→        "clusters": {},
    84→        "superseded": {},
    85→    }
    86→
    87→
    88→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    89→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    90→
    91→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    92→    """
    93→    defaults = empty_plan()
    94→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    95→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    96→
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    98→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   100→        plan["deferred"] = []
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   102→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   104→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   105→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   106→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   107→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   108→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   109→
   110→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   111→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   112→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   113→    if deferred:
   114→        now = utc_now()
   115→        for fid in list(deferred):
   116→            if fid not in skipped:
   117→                skipped[fid] = {
   118→                    "finding_id": fid,
   119→                    "kind": "temporary",
   120→                    "reason": None,
   121→                    "note": None,
   122→                    "attestation": None,
   123→                    "created_at": now,
   124→                    "review_after": None,
   125→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   126→                }
   127→        deferred.clear()
   128→
   129→    # Normalize cluster fields
   130→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   131→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   132→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   133→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   134→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   135→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   136→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   137→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   138→
   139→
   140→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   141→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   142→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   143→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   144→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   145→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   146→
   147→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   148→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   149→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   150→    if overlap:
   151→        raise ValueError(
   152→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   153→        )
   154→
   155→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   156→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   157→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   158→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   159→            raise ValueError(
   160→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   161→            )
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = [
   165→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   166→    "Cluster",
   167→    "ItemOverride",
   168→    "PlanModel",
   169→    "SkipEntry",
   170→    "SupersededEntry",
   171→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   172→    "empty_plan",
   173→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   174→    "validate_plan",
   175→]
   176→
```

```
1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    24→
    25→
    26→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    27→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    28→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    29→    if not plan_path.exists():
    30→        return empty_plan()
    31→
    32→    try:
    33→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    34→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    35→        print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    36→        return empty_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    39→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    40→        return empty_plan()
    41→
    42→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    43→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    44→        print(
    45→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    46→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    47→            file=sys.stderr,
    48→        )
    49→
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    51→    try:
    52→        validate_plan(data)
    53→    except ValueError as ex:
    54→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    55→        return empty_plan()
    56→
    57→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    58→
    59→
    60→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    61→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    62→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    63→
    64→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    65→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    66→    validate_plan(plan)
    67→
    68→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    69→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→
    71→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    72→
    73→    if plan_path.exists():
    74→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    75→        try:
    76→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    77→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    78→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    82→    except OSError as ex:
    83→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
    84→        raise
    85→
    86→
    87→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    88→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    89→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    90→
    91→
    92→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
    93→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
    94→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return False
    97→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    98→    return bool(
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→__all__ = [
   106→    "PLAN_FILE",
   107→    "has_living_plan",
   108→    "load_plan",
   109→    "plan_path_for_state",
   110→    "save_plan",
   111→]
   112→
```
